# Forge Legacy — UX Sweep, November 2026

**Date:** 2026-11-09 · **Code audited:** lane `f705f9d2` (`feat/build11`, = OTA update #5) and the first-launch bundle `e33f79c6` (09-29, embedded in build 11).
**After the audit:** updates #6–#9 (the B9 launch-splash fix, `5ffccf67`) shipped on 11-09. The two findings they touch were re-checked against `5ffccf67` (UX-020 is only partly fixed; L1b-10 is fixed and dropped).

**Method**
- 24 lanes: 18 screen lanes (L1a–L14), plus specialists for the new-athlete journey (S1), the Free tier and money (S2), design system (S3a), accessibility (S3b), copy (S4, code only) and cross-account journeys (S5).
- Each screen was read in code, then run live on the web build (WebKit and Chromium) at 320 / SE / iPhone 14 / Pro Max, in **both themes** (Forge dark and Alabaster light), plus one pass with the phone's appearance set opposite to the app theme.
- Every state was forced where possible: empty, populated, loading, error (fault injection), offline, gated, extreme data.
- 503 screen-states scored on the 12-point Forge Screen Standard (SWEEP/RUBRIC.md). 282 pass (56%).
- 74 findings re-checked by independent verifiers (none failed to reproduce; 16 were lowered). The 3 Highs no verifier took were checked in code by the lead.
- About 558 screenshots were read cold by "five-second" readers (what is this screen, what do I do).

**Accounts:** `qa1109-01`…`-15` (01–03 FREE, 04 Premium without AI, 05 comped Premium, 06–15 Premium by today's default tier; 13, 14 and 15 were created through the real sign-up screens, 15 on the live web preview), `claudetest` (Premium AI, populated, read-mostly), `sandbox` (program and squad second member).
**AI calls:** 15 of the 50 budgeted (L7b 9, L14 4, L11 2). The server refused every one before a model ran (claudetest's `coach_ai` is off; the L11 accounts have no AI), so **$0 was spent** and no real model output was judged.

## Verdict
- **Not ready to press Release as it stands.** 7 Blockers, all OTA- or SQL-sized: 3 are privacy (progress photos can be listed and outlive their deletion; a settings read blip can widen who sees your profile), 4 lose or corrupt data (a Meal Plan week, a running program, a kg athlete's "Continue", an open workout).
- **The biggest launch risk sits outside the OTA:** a new App Store install runs its whole first session on the 09-29 bundle (UX-009), so the 11-08 fixes miss brand-new athletes until a second cold start. That needs a PO decision.
- **The core works.** All 21 first-athlete tap-count tasks meet or beat target, onboarding is clean in both themes, and 282 of 503 screen-states pass.
- **The weak spots are systemic, not scattered:** a failed read looks like "you have nothing" on about 55 screens, offline saves say "Not signed in", Alabaster text fails contrast on about 120 screens, and kg athletes see pounds in 9 places.
- **Totals after verification:** 7 Blocker · 34 High · 147 Medium · 157 Polish (345 findings from 468 lane reports). 283 new, 57 already known, 5 regressions of items marked fixed.

## BEFORE RELEASE
The cut: every Blocker, every first-session High that an OTA or SQL can fix, the regressions of items you counted as done, and the PO decisions that block them. Each is one line here; detail is in the tables.

**Blockers**
- **UX-007** Anyone, without an account, can list the progress-photo files. *SQL:* scope or drop the bucket's select policy; photos still load by URL.
- **UX-001** Deleting a progress set (or a squad post) leaves its photos public, while the confirm says "permanently removed". *OTA:* delete the files with the row, plus a one-time cleanup.
- **UX-003** If a settings read blips, the screen shows defaults and the next tap saves them over the athlete's real choices, which can widen Profile Visibility. *OTA.*
- **UX-002** Meal Plan: one failed read silently rebuilds and saves over the week (locks, logged meals, a cleared week). *OTA.*
- **UX-006** Holt's START IT NOW ends the running program without asking when the "active program" read is slow (>4 s) or fails. *OTA: fail closed.*
- **UX-004** Kg athletes: "Continue this workout" brings sets back as pounds and writes a false PR. *OTA.*
- **UX-005** "Continue this workout" from history silently replaces a workout that is still open. *OTA.*

**First-session Highs**
- **UX-009** **PO decision:** a new install's first session runs the 09-29 bundle (old sign-up, B3 and B8 still live). Build 12 with a launch wait, or release knowing it.
- **UX-008** iPhone SE: the first set is hidden under the footer when an exercise opens (09-26 workout-15 regression). *OTA.*
- **UX-010** Alabaster: the selected chip on Log Food, Food Detail and Log from Photo can't be read (1.4:1). *OTA, colour only.*
- **UX-011** Two Holt doors do nothing (BUILD IT WITH ME; "Ask Coach Holt" on All Programs), then Holt pops up on the next tab. *OTA, one line each.*
- **UX-012** Free: "Start over", "Adjust" or "Ask me again" on Holt's draft burns the one lifetime Holt program. *OTA: spend on keep.*
- **UX-013** Squad invites never mention the iPhone app (09-26 visualB-12). *OTA + web deploy:* App Store link in the text and on the web join page. Universal links wait for build 12.
- **UX-014** Kg athletes: goals are saved in pounds and a typed starting weight is logged as a lb weigh-in. *OTA.*
- **UX-015** HOLD TO SEAL has no VoiceOver / Switch Control path; on the first Workout Complete it is the only way on. *OTA, ~10 lines.*

**Regressions (you counted these as fixed)**
- **UX-016** Offline, Finish never reaches the offline queue and says "Not signed in.." (Launch-QA "Checked and working"). *OTA: one requireUserId helper.*
- **UX-017** The training-week allowance never reaches the app, so B5's guards can't fire. *SQL:* restore `shortPrograms` in `my_entitlement`.
- **UX-018** H5 is back: an accepted ask that never became a session tags the next solo workout "Trained with". *OTA.*
- **UX-034** Route-map pins still say "the first 200 m are trimmed for privacy" (09-26 F5). *OTA, copy.*

**Safe to ride along in the same OTA (one file each, low risk, not required)**
- UX-043 + UX-046 Alabaster ink: one Paper-only token pass (bronzeText, gray600, tab labels). Forge stays pixel-identical.
- UX-031 The toast swallows every tap for 2–3 s (it lives in a full-screen Modal). One file.
- UX-072 Let the app theme drive the status bar and keyboard. JS only; confirm on the phone.

**Already on the release-day checklist (not new):** paste `pending-0259.sql` (P-6 private defaults), the Supabase redirect URL, the landing site's "Free during the beta", and flipping `default_tier` to FREE.

## Top 10
1. **UX-009** — New installs run the 09-29 bundle for their whole first session. Every 11-08 fix misses the people it was for.
2. **UX-007** — Progress photos can be listed by anyone, and (UX-001) survive deletion. The two together expose body photos.
3. **UX-003** — A read blip on Settings saves defaults over real choices, including who can see the profile.
4. **UX-019** — A failed read looks like real data on about 55 screens: "Nothing planned", the day-0 Squads pitch, "Your first plan". Two of its cousins are Blockers (UX-002, UX-003).
5. **UX-006** — Holt can end a running program for good on a slow signal.
6. **UX-005** — "Continue this workout" loses logged sets (and UX-004 writes false PRs for kg athletes).
7. **UX-016** — Offline finishing never queues, and the app tells signed-in athletes they are signed out. A regression of an 11-08 check.
8. **UX-043** — Alabaster: bronze and gray text tokens fail contrast on about 120 screens (UX-046, UX-010). The PO asked for light mode; it is one token pass.
9. **UX-021** — Kg athletes see pounds in 9 places, and three of them write wrong data (UX-004, UX-014, this one).
10. **UX-012** — Free athletes lose their lifetime Holt program to a "Start over" tap; Free limits are frozen at launch (UX-030).

## Systemic fixes (the few changes that fix the most)
| # | Change | Fixes | Effort | When |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Readers throw on error; one `<QueryState>` (skeleton, error with Try again, empty, data); useQuery keeps the last good data when a refetch fails. | UX-019, UX-002, UX-003, UX-165, UX-020 (part) (26 lane findings) | M | The two write-back Blockers before release; Home, Squads, planner, Progress in week 1 |
| 2 | One `requireUserId()` for writers that reads the session locally and treats a network failure as offline, not signed out. | UX-016, UX-019 (part), UX-169 (8 lane findings) | S | Before release (regression) |
| 3 | Alabaster ink and selection: a `bronzeText` role (Paper = bronzeInk), darker Paper gray600/gray400, tab labels on labelInk, selected = selectedFill + accentBorder + selectedInk. | UX-043, UX-046, UX-010, UX-073, UX-087 (27 lane findings) | S | Paper-only; safe before release |
| 4 | Units at the read: one formatter for history weights and distances; never compare lb with kg. | UX-004, UX-021, UX-014, UX-023, UX-140, UX-090, UX-053, UX-126, UX-141 | M | Data-writing ones before release, display ones week 1 |
| 5 | Shared composites once: Toast without a Modal and with an error tone; Button text variant centred at 44 pt; BottomSheet scrolls when it overflows; ConfirmSheet with typed-confirm and keyboard lift; AppBar back 44 pt. | UX-031, UX-087, UX-196, UX-125, UX-159, UX-150 | S | Toast before release; the rest week 1 |
| 6 | Destructive actions confirm or undo; Back asks before discarding typed work. | UX-022, UX-037, UX-171, UX-106, UX-079, UX-039, UX-103, UX-304 | S–M | Week 1 |
| 7 | Entitlement truth: refetch after every create, delete, join, leave and Holt spend; spend Holt's allowance on keep; restore `shortPrograms`; adopted Forge templates don't count. | UX-030, UX-012, UX-017, UX-145, UX-069, UX-149 | S | S2-02 and S2-04 before release; the rest week 1 |
| 8 | Holt placement: one `overlayOpen` flag, a reserved bottom inset for the coin, and every `openCoach()` lands on a home surface. | UX-044, UX-011, UX-114 | S | Doors before release; placement week 1 |
| 9 | Accessibility primitives: un-nest tappables, HoldToConfirm, SheetFrame (modal + escape), `announce()`, one Reduce Motion hook, Dynamic Type caps, a `<Tap>` that guarantees 44 pt. | UX-032, UX-015, UX-088, UX-151, UX-089, UX-074, UX-042 | S each | HoldToConfirm before release; the rest week 1 |
| 10 | Calendar days in local time (one date helper, client-supplied local date on writes). | UX-045 (6 lane findings) + 09-26 B14 | S (SQL + JS) | Week 1 |

Smaller themes, all week 1 or backlog: raw server text (UX-158), saves that fail silently or off-screen (UX-080, UX-081), the app theme driving the OS chrome (UX-072), one primary button (UX-156), a type ramp and spacing scale (S3a SYS8, backlog), one terms list enforced by a test (UX-157).

## New-athlete journey
S1 ran `qa1109-01` (FREE) from a fresh device through sign-in, 7-step onboarding, the Free paywall, Home, Strength Foundation I (3 sessions), a first squad, goal, food, Legacy and Holt's first nudge, in both themes.

**Tap counts vs the 09-26 baseline (iPhone 14)**
| Task | 09-26 | 11-09 | Target | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Start a beginner program | 4 (For You led with Advanced) | 4 | ≤ 4 | Meets; For You now leads with Beginner. Spends a Free slot silently (UX-069). |
| Log today's workout | 1 | 1 | 1 | Meets |
| Log a past run | 3 | 3 | ≤ 3 | Meets; a date is now chosen (H26). Not offered on the Start sheet. |
| Find my bench PR | 3 + ~5 long scrolls (dead end) | 3 | ≤ 3 | Fixed: Library search → exercise → Your history. The rank-pill route still hides the bench (S1-12). |
| Change to kg | 2 (found by guessing) | 3 | ≤ 3 | Meets (Avatar → Preferences → Kgs). "Kgs" isn't a unit (S4-13). |
| Talk to the coach | 1 | 1 | 1 | Meets |
| Create a squad | 1–2 | 2 | ≤ 2 | Meets; the new squad has no Invite button (UX-071). |
| See my honors | 1 + 3 scrolls | 1 | ≤ 2 | Beats (Home HONORS). |
| Build my own program | 3 (unlabelled +) | 3 | ≤ 3 | Meets; + is labelled "Create new". |
| Find a past workout | 3 ("Reference" easy to miss) | 3 | ≤ 3 | Meets; "Reference" is gone. |
| Add a progress photo | 3 (wrong album) | 2 | ≤ 3 | Meets; opens a 6-pose set form for what may be one photo. |
| Notification settings | 2 | 2 | ≤ 2 | Meets |
| Get help | 2 + scroll (no help centre) | 2 | ≤ 2 | Meets the count, but it opens Holt's generic intro, and some answers are wrong (UX-063). |
| See every rank | 3 + scrolls | 2 | ≤ 3 | Beats. |
| Find Friends | not reachable from Squads | 1 (Home) / 2 (Squads) | ≤ 2 | Fixed. |
| Log a food | — | 3 (+1 consent once) | ≤ 3 | Meets. |
| Log a 3×5 squat (freestyle) | — | 12 (13 with Finish) | ≤ 12 | Meets only by stepping the default 3×8 down. |
| Finish onboarding | — | 13 taps, 18 s | — | Clean; ends on the plans screen (+1 "Continue with Free"). |
| Log a whole program session (21 sets) | — | 41 taps | — | 8 of them close Holt's bubble (~24%, UX-044). |

**Words a newcomer meets unexplained** (definitions in guidance-pack.md)
- **Chapter / Seal:** onboarding explains Chapter, but on SE the text is scrolled away (UX-055). "Hold to Seal" on the first Workout Complete reads like closing the chapter that just began.
- **Honor, Legacy, Foundation I (rank), Under Iron, Volume (no unit), Mission, Your Circle, Pinned Legacy, Featured Legacy Moment:** each was defined only by the retired tour.
- **Prev, Main lift, Best, STAY / MINIMISE** in the logger; **Acknowledge** (Respect / Honor / Support / Strength) on posts; **Basic Holt vs Holt AI** on the paywall; **Kgs**; **Forging since, Lifetime, Minutes forged, Best streak** on Progress.

**Where a first-time athlete gets stuck or loses heart**
- A full paywall before the first workout (FREE; UX-070); on the web it can't sell (UX-164).
- Legacy says "Start Your First Chapter" after everything said Chapter I is open (UX-047).
- The first Workout Complete: HOLD TO SEAL is 2.6:1 in Alabaster (UX-043), can't be done with VoiceOver (UX-015), then five honor ceremonies in a row (UX-210).
- Back on Home, the finished session is replaced by the next one under "Today's Workout", with no "done for today" (UX-068).
- Free limits spent without a word: a lifetime program slot on Start, the only squad on Create (UX-069).
- A goal named after a lift never moves by itself (UX-050); a new squad has no Invite button (UX-071).
- Holt's bubble sits on controls; closing it is about a quarter of a session's taps (UX-044). Day-0 headline cut to "Start Your First …" (UX-057).

## Release-day delta (what launch users will see that testers don't)
**1. FREE by default.** Every tester, and every `qa1109` account except 01–03, is Premium through today's `default_tier`. When it flips to FREE (checklist §6), new athletes meet what only S1, S2 and three Free accounts saw:
- the paywall as the first screen after setup (UX-070), silent spends of lifetime slots (UX-069);
- the Holt allowance traps (UX-012, UX-186), limits frozen at launch (UX-030), an invisible training-week allowance (UX-017), Forge templates eating the 5-template allowance (UX-145);
- "See Premium AI" opening the Premium tab (UX-146), and a trial promised to Apple IDs that will be charged (UX-040).
**2. P-6 privacy (0259 on release day).** New sign-ups become private by default; testers keep the open 0189 "testing posture" (profiles, live set-by-set view, squad training alerts on). So testers see more of each other than launch users will, and the Train Together and "Your circle" paths were tested on open profiles.
- UX-003 is unaffected by 0259: it writes the app's client-side defaults over the stored row, whatever the server default is.
**3. The first-launch bundle (UX-009).** Testers' phones already run the newest OTA. A new install runs e33f79c6 (09-29) until its second cold start:
- old sign-up (no 8-character check; a reset accepts "bad"), "Begin Chapter I" on Welcome, the old Settings copy ("On by default while we're testing", a false "We attach the screen you were on");
- B3 (End Walk → Cancel throws the walk away) and B8 (a weight-loss goal achieved on its first update) are still live there; "They is training · Let's go them!".
**4. Invites land on the web.** Every invite link opens forgelegacy.expo.app (UX-013), where the paywall can't sell (UX-164) and the signed-out landing never mentions the invite (UX-078).

## All findings by severity
Columns: id (lane reports merged) · title · screens · what · fix idea · where · reach · gate · fix type · known (vs Launch-QA 11-08 and Full-App-QA 09-26) · verified. Full text for each is in findings.json.

### Blocker (7)

**Rows still flagged after closing** (L10 is the only lane that listed them; each is a filed finding):
- squad-settings.tsx:739-742 ❌ Mute Squad + 3 switches are device-only, read by nothing (UX-092)
- squad/[id].tsx:394,414 ⚠ removal doesn't keep the member out (UX-035)
- squad/[id].tsx:1230 ⚠ Save grey with no reason (UX-052)
- squad/[id].tsx:1236 ⚠ Remove goal one tap; Past Goals unreachable (UX-304)
- squad/[id]/goal.tsx:179,206 ⚠ replace stacks a 2nd Squad Detail (UX-162)
- squad-settings.tsx:116 ⚠ stale owner Settings after Transfer (UX-162)
- squad-transfer.tsx:84 ⚠ replace leaves stale Settings + duplicate squad (UX-162)
- create-squad.tsx:117 ⚠ Back discards input (UX-079)
- discover-squads.tsx:377 ⚠ Request Sent withdraws silently / commitment dead-end (known)
- squad-invite.tsx:317 ⚠ Copied claimed on failure (known)

**Route ownership:** 121 of 121 route files owned by a lane (none unowned). `workout.tsx` is shared by L2a and L2b. 22 web-only forks (`*.web.*`) were judged by code.

**Theme coverage.** Every screen was opened and read in both Forge (dark) and Alabaster (light). 251 of 503 screen-states recorded an Alabaster difference; 252 were the same. These states were run in Forge only (the code path is the same in both themes):
- L1b Home — failed reads / slow backend (faults)
- L2a Logger — extremes at 320 (long names, 9999 lb)
- L3a Workout Complete reached from Activity Detail (owned by L3b)
- L3b Workout Complete review after a reload
- L6 Guided builder and Program Builder — save failed / offline
- L7b Typed Holt resilience states (queue, cut-off, reload, offline)
- L7b Form Check real refusals and stop
- L8 Chapter Detail — read failure
- L8 Legacy on the first-launch bundle
- L11 Write a Workout — edit mode
- L11 Squad Post — error / not found
- L11 Squad Composer — Compose Workout
- L12 Account Settings, Edit Profile, Subscription — read failures
- L13 Create Food — edit a custom food
- L14 Nutrition Details and Meal Plan — read failures
- S1 Holt nudge after 3 sessions (shows once per 7 days)
- S3b Dynamic Type simulation (contrast was measured in both)

### Light mode (Alabaster)
The PO asked for light mode explicitly. What the sweep found, in order of how much it matters:
- **Text too pale (the big one).** bronze300 is used as text on about 120 screens at 2.2–2.6:1 (UX-043), and gray600 tertiary text fails in 127 places at 2.8–3.5:1, with the tab labels at 3.5:1 on every screen (UX-046). One Paper-only token pass fixes both; Forge doesn't change.
- **Selected states unreadable.** Solid bronzeDark chips with dark ink (1.4:1) on Log Food, Food Detail, Log from Photo and the Transformation date picker (UX-010). There are six looks for "selected"; Alabaster's rule is selectedFill + accentBorder + labelInk.
- **Field vs disabled:** an empty text field and a disabled button are the same recessed taupe plate (UX-073). Five-second readers kept reading placeholders as typed values.
- **Toasts** are a cream pill on cream cards; one icon is white on cream (UX-087).
- **Dark leftovers:** a black share card and a blank bronze square in the Save-image export (UX-340), share keepsakes that disagree on theme (UX-343), cold black scrims on nine overlays (UX-294), Forge-only literals on Progress (UX-289).
- **OS chrome** follows the phone, not the app: Alabaster on a Dark-mode iPhone gets light status-bar glyphs on cream and a dark keyboard (UX-072; phone check).
- **Other Alabaster-only items:** featured and unfeatured accomplishments look the same (UX-283); changing theme mid-onboarding wipes the answers (UX-318); Holt's "free days used" line is 2.5:1 (UX-186); in the logger Holt's "Use 135 lb" pill is dark on dark (seen by L2a, part of UX-010's fix).
- What was clean in Alabaster: layouts are identical, no dark cards or sheets are left on the main paths, and ceremonies and sheets render cream. With the phone in Dark Mode, an Alabaster launch shows a dark frame for about 150–250 ms before the cream page (part of UX-072); no white flashes were seen.

| ID | Severity | Alabaster finding |
|---|---|---|
| UX-010 | High | Alabaster: selected chips and pills are dark brown on dark brown and can't be read (Log Food's filter, Food Detail's unit and meal, Log from Photo, the Transformation date picker) |
| UX-043 | Medium | Alabaster: bronze300 is used as text (2.2–2.6:1) on about 120 screens, including HOLD TO SEAL, ADD SET, Add Friend/Accept, the invite code and the paywall's Premium column |
| UX-046 | Medium | Alabaster: gray600 tertiary text (2.8–3.5:1) in 127 places on 15 of the 16 top screens, and the tab-bar labels (3.5–3.6:1) on every screen |
| UX-073 | Medium | Alabaster: an empty text field and a disabled button are the same recessed taupe plate |
| UX-087 | Medium | Failures are announced in the success toast, and the logger and the picker each have their own toast |
| UX-186 | Medium | Coach Holt's "free days used" refusal, which comes after the questions, has no way to Premium, and in Alabaster its only sentence is 2.5:1 |
| UX-197 | Polish | Each ceremony draws its own mark and its own button |
| UX-283 | Polish | Alabaster: featured and unfeatured accomplishments look the same in the list, and the star isn't announced |
| UX-289 | Polish | Alabaster: Forge-only literals on Progress (black What's Next shadow, invisible chart gridlines) |
| UX-294 | Polish | Nine hand-rolled overlays use a cold black scrim instead of the theme's overlay, so in Alabaster they lay a grey smog over cream |
| UX-318 | Polish | Changing the theme mid-onboarding wipes every answer typed so far, and picking Forge from Alabaster asks the theme question again |
| UX-340 | Polish | Save image export differs from the preview: blank bronze square instead of the Forge mark, and a black card in Alabaster |
| UX-343 | Polish | Share keepsakes disagree on theme: the Honor and Transformation cards turn cream in Alabaster, the Progress Photo card stays black |

## Not tested (and why)
| Area | What | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Device-only | Push delivery and taps, haptics, camera and photo picker, GPS in the background, StoreKit purchase and restore, HealthKit, the watch, Live Activities, iOS keyboard, safe areas, VoiceOver, Switch Control, Dynamic Type, Smart Invert, universal links and Messages linkification | The web build can't run them. Each is in phone-script-additions.md. |
| The App Store first launch | A real first install moving from the embedded bundle to the OTA, and the Alabaster reload swapping bundles mid-onboarding | expo-updates is device-only; the two bundles were run side by side on :8091 and :8090 instead. |
| Real AI output | Holt typed answers, Kitchen, form-check reads, meal-photo and recipe-photo reads, program photo import | claudetest's coach_ai is off server-side, so every call was refused before a model ran ($0). Flows ran on mocked replies. |
| Hard rules | Workouts on claudetest or sandbox, the final Delete Account tap, public squads, real public Discover/Request/Approve, sharing a community food, friend requests to real people | Sweep safety rules. Each was run up to the last step, or against mocked RPCs. |
| Data that takes days | A finished competition (podium, Hall, Trophy Case), a real weekly recap, multi-day server history, the nutrition care line, squad goal met | Nothing could finish today; judged on sample payloads. L11 left SQL that closes "QA1109 Daily Grind" if the PO wants to see it. |
| Free tier on most lanes | Free caps on the builders, photos (75), videos (5) and the planner | Only 01–03 are Free; the rest are Premium by default_tier. Gates were traced in code. |
| Slow-network cold loads | Chromium CPU×4 + slow 3G from a cold start | The web bundle alone takes 30–120 s on slow 3G; the phone ships its bundle. In-app navigation and backend latency were timed instead. |
| Web gestures | Swipe paging, swipe-to-delete, wheel flicks, pinch/drag photo framing, drag-to-reorder | Mouse drags don't drive RN gestures in WebKit; long-press paths were used where they exist. |
| Two-account device states | A queued offline save carried across an account switch | Offline Finish never queues on head (UX-016), so there was nothing to carry. |
| Retired or gated code | The retired tour's spotlights, Start Day 1 reveal, Schedule step, Guided tips toggle | Switched off in code (TOUR_RETIRED, HOLT_FIRST_WEEK = false). |

- **Accounts created by the sweep:** `qa1109-13` and `qa1109-14` (real sign-up on head and on the first-launch bundle), `qa1109-15` (sign-up on the live web from an invite). All Premium by default_tier.
- **Moderation queue: 3 reports, all on QA1109 content.** L10: squad 6af73005 ("Something else", note says QA test) and post 0cd5e60a ("Spam or a scam"), both filed by sandbox. L11: qa1109-07 reported qa1109-09's announcement 404b5348 ("Spam or a scam").

L10's three squads were deleted in-app.
- **Workouts, programs, templates:** many QA1109 workouts on 01, 04, 06, 07, 11, 12 (06 has a 26-hour test session); programs on 01, 03, 04 (04's "8-Week Muscle Block" was ended early by the UX-006 test), 08; templates on 03 (5 adopted starters), 07, 08; a week template on 08.
- **Legacy:** qa1109-10 has three sealed chapters, 13 goals, 4 accomplishments, a Standard, pins and a 190 lb weigh-in; qa1109-12 has an achieved "Cut to 175 lb" goal and two weigh-ins.
- **Nutrition:** food entries, user foods and saved meals on 02, 04, 01, 07; consents in mixed states on 02, 05, 10.
- **Friends and invites:** 11 and 12 are friends; two accepted workout_invites rows (1d70d9f6, 38fd6fef) and notification rows among 11, 12 and 15.
- **claudetest (populated tester):** the "Never Alone" honor was claimed by a /honors visit (from its real data, celebrated_at set 11-09 01:50 UTC), and L7b's mocked Kitchen run left three kitchen_suggestions in Holt's 30-day memory ("Garlic Chicken Rice Bowl", "Sheet-Pan Chicken and Broccoli", "Chicken Fried Rice"). Theme, units, visibility and program unchanged.
- **sandbox:** a soft-deleted custom exercise "QA1109 Sweep Lift Renamed" (invisible). Program, home gym and favourites restored.
- **Storage:** squad-media holds QA1109 test images and orphaned composer uploads under squad feaf32ff; L9 deleted its transformation-media test files.
- **Device-local only:** about 25 abandoned in-browser sessions (no server rows); possible stale live-presence rows on 01 and 06.

## Appendix A: dropped and corrected findings
**Not reproduced:** none. All 74 verifier checks reproduced the core defect; 16 were lowered in severity (listed in the Verified column).

**Fixed after the lane commit (dropped):**
- L1b-10 "Launch is not one picture: tab bar and Holt coin appear over the splash" — Update #6 (43b71a6e, 11-09) took the Holt coin off the boot screen (CoachBubble returns null while the profile loads). The tab-bar-over-splash half was not re-checked; look for it on the phone.

**Claims the verifiers corrected (the finding stays, this part is dropped):**
- S1-01 (UX-009): Example B4 is not in the 09-29 bundle (no "Discard workout" row there); B3 is. Replaced.
- L1a-01 (UX-009): "Alabaster links at ~2.4:1" can't happen on a fresh install (Forge until the Theme step). Onboarding barely differs between bundles.
- S1-02 (UX-043): "A tap does nothing": a real tap shows "Keep holding…" and a sliver of fill for ~0.3 s (the lane used a Playwright click). Contrast stands.
- F-L5-01 (UX-011): Settings → Help, Holt Memory and Form Check already work; only two doors are dead.
- L7a-18 (UX-042): NEW CHAT / CLOSE (52 pt) and the coin-line X (46 pt) are fine on device; react-native-web ignores hitSlop.
- F-L5-02 (UX-038): "No max known → no way in" is overstated: "From a set" or a guess still gets you in.
- L6-12 (UX-042): The Workout Builder comparison was wrong (its controls are 30 and 34×38 pt, not 42); both builders are under 44.
- L11-08 (UX-019): The "bad link → check your connection" half applies only to malformed ids; a deleted post with a real id says "It may have been deleted".
- L12-01 (UX-003): The trigger is narrower: a failed read followed by a successful write. Fully offline, the write fails too.
- L3b-03 (UX-116): A PR or honor card doesn't add height (it replaces the quote and hero); "Up next" plus a long name does.
- L13-02 (UX-165): A single hiccup never re-asks; it takes two failed reads in a row, and a decline is the privacy-safe side.
- L4-08 (UX-043): "Decline" has a different root (0.55 opacity), and "No athletes found" clears 3:1.
- L3a-01 (UX-023): The Apple Health part is weaker: it follows mi/km; it only ignores the swim/row scale.
- L14-12 (UX-313): Not a code defect: the refusal matches the .dc and the status archive; the docs disagree. PO decision.

**Not filed by the lanes (judged artefacts):** a mid-workout sign-out on a faked "day 2" (client clock 2 days ahead → token refresh 429; S1); React error #418 on first web load (known 09-26 B2).

**Not bugs (PO rulings):** five-second readers flagged "the Legacy tab tile is lit on every tab" about a dozen times. The PO reversed QA home-04 on 09-29: the tile stays lit on every tab. Not filed.

**Merges:** 468 lane reports became 345 findings; 122 were merged into another with the same root cause. Every lane id is kept in its finding's first column.

About 558 screenshots were shown cold to readers who answered: what is this, what would you do, how clear (0–2). Approximate split: **clarity 2 ≈ 402 (72%) · 1 ≈ 129 (23%) · 0 ≈ 27 (5%).**

| other | 1 | 1 | 0 | 0 |

**Clarity 0 (≈27)** was mostly a frame with nothing to read:
- blank boot or theme-switch frames with the phone's appearance opposite to the app (8; UX-072);
- a bare spinner with no title (about 9: Legacy, Chapter, a profile, a post, a squad, Rank Progression, Progress). S3a counted 84 bare spinners against 2 skeletons;
- a failure with no way forward (5: "Couldn't load progress." with no Retry, Add a Photo "no chapter is open" with no button, the planner gate's blank page, Edit Profile spinning; all UX-019);
- the pinned-video player's black screen (UX-339), and a live workout page that spins for ever after a block (new candidate, below).

**What readers stumbled on most often** (approximate count of screenshots):
- Holt's bubble covering something (~20; UX-044).
- A placeholder read as a typed value (~15: "IRON-7Q2K", "500", "marcusvale", "The Comeback", "DELETE", "Restaurant lunch", "3.1", "Bench Press / 315 lb"; UX-073, UX-052).
- Rank and Progress words: "Foundation I", "Forging since", "Lifetime 1", "Minutes forged" (~12; guidance pack).
- A main button disabled with no reason (~10).
- The paywall with nothing to buy on web (~10; UX-164).
- "Competitio / ns" breaking mid-word (6; UX-189) and "Start Your First Chapter" when Chapter I exists (5; UX-047).
- "Seal" and "Under Iron" (5), "Acknowledge" vs "Respect" for the same button (4), no Invite on a new squad (3; UX-071).

**New candidates the five-second reads raised (not filed by any lane; worth a look in week 1):**
- Squads tab: for an athlete already in a squad, the biggest button is still "Create a Squad", above their own squad card (4 readers, clarity 1).
- Opening a "commented on your post" notification shows the post but not the comment (S5 j7, clarity 1).
- The live-workout page after a block shows only a spinner and never says it can't be shown (S5 j5-14, clarity 0).
- An "Earned First Workout Logged" ceremony right after tapping Start Program, before anything was logged (S5 j1; likely the queued ceremony from an earlier session).
- The seal summary dates a chapter "Nov 9 – Nov 8" (backwards; part of UX-045).
- The planned-workout preview says "7 exercises" over a list of 9, and shows "Push-Up 5 × 100" (S1 w1, L1b).
